{"parentUuid":null,"isSidechain":true,"promptId":"3bfd3d93-3d8c-4d5a-8244-149007afff7e","agentId":"ae42573080b47b5cd","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"Deeply study the AGENT EXECUTION CORE and SANDBOX RUNTIME of Strix (an open-source agentic AI pentest tool) at /tmp/claude-1000/-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main/7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27/scratchpad/strix. I'm assessing folding its core ideas into a fused security+personal-AI system (SIGIL + CRUCIBLE).\n\nRead and explain in DEPTH:\n1. **The agent loop** — strix/core/{agents.py,runner.py,execution.py,sessions.py,hooks.py,inputs.py,paths.py} and strix/agents/{factory.py,prompt.py,__init__.py}. How does an agent get constructed and run? It uses the OpenAI Agents SDK (`openai-agents[litellm]`) — how is the agent, its tools, and its loop wired to that SDK? How are LLM turns executed, how is tool-calling dispatched, how does the agent decide to continue/finish? What is the concurrency model (asyncio)? How are hooks used?\n2. **Multi-agent orchestration** — strix/tools/agents_graph/tools.py + strix/core/agents.py. How does an agent SPAWN sub-agents (the \"agents graph\")? Parent/child relationships, message passing, coordination, termination.\n3. **The Docker Kali sandbox** — strix/runtime/{docker_client.py,session_manager.py,backends.py,caido_bootstrap.py,local_dir_staging.py} + containers/Dockerfile + containers/docker-entrypoint.sh. How is the sandbox provisioned and managed? What tools/binaries are in the Kali image? How does the agent execute shell commands + tools INSIDE the container? How is the target-app / workspace mounted/staged? What is the isolation/security model of the sandbox? How are multiple sessions managed?\n4. **The execution/runner control flow** — trace one full run: from `strix/interface/main.py` → runner → agent construction → sandbox spin-up → agent loop → tool calls in the sandbox → finish. Note the key classes/functions and their responsibilities.\n5. **State + config** — strix/config/{loader.py,models.py,settings.py}: how are LLM providers/models/keys configured (litellm)? What are the key settings?\n\nBe concrete with file:line references and quote key code. Produce a clear mental model of \"how Strix works\" at the execution+sandbox level. {"parentUuid":"f4aac57a-2e7f-4741-9260-d2140709879c","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ae42573080b47b5cd","attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- crucible: How to operate CRUCIBLE — the authorized, prove-don't-guess offensive-security engine in framework/v2. Read this BEFORE running an engagement/scan, re-verifying a finding, inspecting the event spine, reasoning about the veracity/anti-hallucination layer, the learning/metacognition core, or the safety stack. Use it when a task names CRUCIBLE, OBSIDIAN, `python3 -m framework.v2 <subcommand>`, findings/oracles/evidence certificates, the blackboard event spine, critics, reflection, or the reward/calibration loop.\n- deep-research: Deep research harness — fan-out web searches, fetch sources, adversarially verify claims, synthesize a cited report. - When the user wants a deep, multi-source, fact-checked research report on any topic. BEFORE invoking, check if the question is specific enough to research directly — if underspecified (e.g., \"what car to buy\" without budget/use-case/region), ask 2-3 clarifying questions to narrow scope. Then pass the refined question as args, weaving the answers in.\n- adversarial-sweep: Independent multi-lens adversarial re-attack of a defensive-oracle domain — the FORGE stage-9 companion to RED-PEN. Attack from disjoint lenses, refute each objection with a real repro, report only what survives. - Before merging any AEGIS/FORGE domain stream, alongside (not instead of) the red-pen agent. Pass the domain spec via args. On the email-auth stream it caught 3 CRITICAL/LOW defects PAST a RED-PEN PASS — near-zero-FP cannot be self-certified by one reviewer.\n- dataviz: Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: \"chart\", \"graph\", \"plot\", \"data viz\", \"visualization\", \"dashboard\", \"analytics\", \"visualize data\", \"categorical colors\", \"sequential / diverging palette\", \"stat tile\", \"sparkline\", \"heatmap\", \"legend\", \"axis\", \"tooltip\", \"chart colors\", \"color by series\".\n- artifact-design: Design guidance and fundamentals for Artifacts.\n- artifact-capabilities: Runtime capabilities a published Artifact page can be granted — behavior static HTML cannot provide on its own, such as the page reading live or connected data, keeping state shared across viewers, or updating and republishing itself. Serves this user's live capability roster and the typed call definitions. Load it whenever the user asks for an artifact needing any such runtime behavior.\n- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- verify: Verify that a code change actually does what it's supposed to by exercising it end-to-end and observing behavior — drive the affected flow, not just tests or typecheck. Run before committing nontrivial changes; bootstraps this repo's project verify skill if none exists yet. Don't invoke it on a diff that only touches tests, docs, or other code with no runtime surface to drive (a change to product source always has one) — there's nothing to observe.\n- code-review: Review the current diff for correctness bugs and reuse/simplification/efficiency cleanups at the given effort level (low/medium: fewer, high-confidence findings; high→max: broader coverage, may include uncertain findings; ultra: deep multi-agent review in the cloud). Pass --comment to post findings as inline PR comments, or --fix to apply the findings to the working tree after the review.\n- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. The root agent\n566\t        # hits the same limit on its next call and tears the scan down.\n567\t        try:\n568\t            await run_agent_loop(\n569\t                agent=child_agent,\n570\t                initial_input=initial_input,\n571\t                run_config=run_config,\n572\t                context=child_ctx,\n573\t                max_turns=max_turns,\n574\t                coordinator=coordinator,\n575\t                agent_id=child_id,\n576\t                interactive=interactive,\n577\t                session=session,\n578\t                start_parked=start_parked,\n579\t                event_sink=event_sink,\n580\t                hooks=hooks,\n581\t            )\n582\t        except BudgetExceededError:\n583\t            logger.info(\"child %s stopped after reaching the scan budget limit\", child_id)\n584\t\n585\t    task_handle = asyncio.create_task(_child_loop(), name=f\"agent-{name}-{child_id}\")\n586\t    await coordinator.attach_runtime(child_id, task=task_handle)\n587\t"}]},"uuid":"d16c7219-3181-4c29-93d2-c9e3a7259147","timestamp":"2026-07-20T01:20:55.446Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"18cc5c72-4629-499f-8cdb-d7038044a814","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"dca94782-0c29-4c73-9a2a-a0108ff6b04f","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ae42573080b47b5cd","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdCS1LEH3QtWEiqwbtFYW","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01LoPzvPjYmNSqDhXYR8GsEY","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/tmp/claude-1000/-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main/7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27/scratchpad/strix/strix/core/sessions.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":19323,"cache_read_input_tokens":13876,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":19323,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdCS1JRsxticQL7KS8boU","attributionAgent":"Explore","type":"assistant","uuid":"28f7ccc1-2f02-43c4-86f1-c42b0b9ef192","timestamp":"2026-07-20T01:21:01.814Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"28f7ccc1-2f02-43c4-86f1-c42b0b9ef192","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"3bfd3d93-3d8c-4d5a-8244-149007afff7e","agentId":"ae42573080b47b5cd","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01LoPzvPjYmNSqDhXYR8GsEY","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SDK session helpers for Strix agents.\"\"\"\n2\t\n3\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n4\t\n5\timport asyncio\n6\timport logging\n7\tfrom typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any, cast\n8\tfrom weakref import WeakKeyDictionary\n9\t\n10\tfrom agents.memory import SQLiteSession\n11\t\n12\t\n13\tif TYPE_CHECKING:\n14\t    from collections.abc import Callable\n15\t    from pathlib import Path\n16\t\n17\t    from agents.items import TResponseInputItem\n18\t    from agents.memory import Session\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n22\t\n23\t\n24\tdef open_agent_session(agent_id: str, path: Path) -> SQLiteSession:\n25\t    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n26\t    return SQLiteSession(session_id=agent_id, db_path=path)\n27\t\n28\t\n29\t_IMAGE_REJECTED_TEXT = \"[image rejected by the model]\"\n30\t_IMAGE_ELIDED_TEXT = \"[older screenshot elided to bound context memory]\"\n31\t_INHERITED_IMAGE_TEXT = \"[screenshot omitted from inherited context]\"\n32\t\n33\t\n34\tdef _output_has_image(item_dict: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:\n35\t    return (\n36\t        item_dict.get(\"type\") == \"function_call_output\"\n37\t        and isinstance(item_dict.get(\"output\"), list)\n38\t        and any(isinstance(b, dict) and b.get(\"type\") == \"input_image\" for b in item_dict[\"output\"])\n39\t    )\n40\t\n41\t\n42\tdef _elided_output(item_dict: dict[str, Any], text: str) -> dict[str, Any]:\n43\t    # Replace only image blocks; sibling text blocks are preserved.\n44\t    output = item_dict.get(\"output\")\n45\t    blocks = output if isinstance(output, list) else []\n46\t    return {\n47\t        \"type\": \"function_call_output\",\n48\t        \"call_id\": item_dict.get(\"call_id\"),\n49\t        \"output\": [\n50\t            {\"type\": \"input_text\", \"text\": text}\n51\t            if isinstance(block, dict) and block.get(\"type\") == \"input_image\"\n52\t            else block\n53\t            for block in blocks\n54\t        ],\n55\t    }\n56\t\n57\t\n58\t_session_write_locks: WeakKeyDictionary[Session, asyncio.Lock] = WeakKeyDictionary()\n59\t\n60\t\n61\tdef session_write_lock(session: Session) -> asyncio.Lock:\n62\t    \"\"\"Lock serialising all out-of-band writes to ``session``.\"\"\"\n63\t    lock = _session_write_locks.get(session)\n64\t    if lock is None:\n65\t        lock = asyncio.Lock()\n66\t        _session_write_locks[session] = lock\n67\t    return lock\n68\t\n69\t\n70\tasync def _rewrite_session(\n71\t    session: Session,\n72\t    transform: Callable[[list[Any]], tuple[list[Any], bool]],\n73\t) -> bool:\n74\t    \"\"\"Read-modify-write a session under its write lock, restoring on failure.\"\"\"\n75\t    async with session_write_lock(session):\n76\t        items = await session.get_items()\n77\t        if not items:\n78\t            return False\n79\t        rebuilt, changed = transform(list(items))\n80\t        if not changed:\n81\t            return False\n82\t        rebuilt_items = cast(\"list[TResponseInputItem]\", rebuilt)\n83\t        original_items = cast(\"list[TResponseInputItem]\", list(items))\n84\t        await session.clear_session()\n85\t        try:\n86\t            await session.add_items(rebuilt_items)\n87\t        except Exception:\n88\t            logger.exception(\"session rewrite failed; restoring original items\")\n89\t            await session.clear_session()\n90\t            await session.add_items(original_items)\n91\t            raise\n92\t        return True\n93\t\n94\t\n95\tasync def strip_all_images_from_session(session: Session) -> bool:\n96\t    \"\"\"Replace every image tool output with a text placeholder (rejection recovery).\"\"\"\n97\t\n98\t    def _transform(items: list[Any]) -> tuple[list[Any], bool]:\n99\t        rebuilt: list[Any] = []\n100\t        changed = False\n101\t        for item in items:\n102\t            item_dict = cast(\"dict[str, Any]\", item) if isinstance(item, dict) else None\n103\t            if item_dict is not None and _output_has_image(item_dict):\n104\t                rebuilt.append(_elided_output(item_dict, _IMAGE_REJECTED_TEXT))\n105\t                changed = True\n106\t            else:\n107\t                rebuilt.append(item)\n108\t        return rebuilt, changed\n109\t\n110\t    return await _rewrite_session(session, _transform)\n111\t\n112\t\n113\tasync def enforce_image_budget(session: Session, max_images: int) -> bool:\n114\t    \"\"\"Keep only the most recent ``max_images`` image outputs; elide older ones.\"\"\"\n115\t    if max_images < 0:\n116\t        return False\n117\t\n118\t    def _transform(items: list[Any]) -> tuple[list[Any], bool]:\n119\t        image_indices = [\n120\t            i\n121\t            for i, item in enumerate(items)\n122\t            if isinstance(item, dict) and _output_has_image(cast(\"dict[str, Any]\", item))\n123\t        ]\n124\t        if len(image_indices) <= max_images:\n125\t            return items, False\n126\t        to_elide = set(image_indices[: len(image_indices) - max_images])\n127\t        rebuilt = [\n128\t            _elided_output(cast(\"dict[str, Any]\", item), _IMAGE_ELIDED_TEXT)\n129\t            if i in to_elide\n130\t            else item\n131\t            for i, item in enumerate(items)\n132\t        ]\n133\t        return rebuilt, True\n134\t\n135\t    return await _rewrite_session(session, _transform)\n136\t\n137\t\n138\tdef scrub_images_from_items(items: list[Any]) -> list[Any]:\n139\t    \"\"\"Return a copy of ``items`` with every image block replaced by text.\"\"\"\n140\t\n141\t    def _scrub(obj: Any) -> Any:\n142\t        if isinstance(obj, dict):\n143\t            if obj.get(\"type\") == \"input_image\":\n144\t                return {\"type\": \"input_text\", \"text\": _INHERITED_IMAGE_TEXT}\n145\t            return {k: _scrub(v) for k, v in obj.items()}\n146\t        if isinstance(obj, list):\n147\t            return [_scrub(v) for v in obj]\n148\t        return obj\n149\t\n150\t    return [_scrub(item) for item in items]\n151\t"}]},"uuid":"85a02b5e-6397-49f2-bd6e-c499778b5f73","timestamp":"2026-07-20T01:21:01.831Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"28f7ccc1-2f02-43c4-86f1-c42b0b9ef192","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

run hooks used by Strix orchestration.\"\"\"\n2\t\n3\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n4\t\n5\timport logging\n6\timport math\n7\tfrom typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any\n8\t\n9\tfrom agents.lifecycle import RunHooks\n10\t\n11\tfrom strix.report.state import get_global_report_state\n12\t\n13\t\n14\tif TYPE_CHECKING:\n15\t    from agents import RunContextWrapper\n16\t    from agents.agent import Agent\n17\t    from agents.items import ModelResponse\n18\t\n19\t\n20\tlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n21\t\n22\t\n23\tclass BudgetExceededError(RuntimeError):\n24\t    \"\"\"Raised when the accumulated LLM cost reaches the configured budget.\"\"\"\n25\t\n26\t\n27\tclass ReportUsageHooks(RunHooks[dict[str, Any]]):\n28\t    \"\"\"Persist SDK-native usage after every model response.\"\"\"\n29\t\n30\t    def __init__(self, *, model: str, max_budget_usd: float | None = None) -> None:\n31\t        if max_budget_usd is not None and (\n32\t            not math.isfinite(max_budget_usd) or max_budget_usd <= 0\n33\t        ):\n34\t            raise ValueError(\"max_budget_usd must be a finite number greater than 0\")\n35\t        self._model = model\n36\t        self._max_budget_usd = max_budget_usd\n37\t\n38\t    async def on_llm_end(\n39\t        self,\n40\t        context: RunContextWrapper[dict[str, Any]],\n41\t        agent: Agent[dict[str, Any]],\n42\t        response: ModelResponse,\n43\t    ) -> None:\n44\t        report_state = get_global_report_state()\n45\t        if report_state is None:\n46\t            return\n47\t\n48\t        ctx = context.context if isinstance(context.context, dict) else {}\n49\t        agent_name = getattr(agent, \"name\", None)\n50\t        if not isinstance(agent_name, str):\n51\t            agent_name = None\n52\t        agent_id = ctx.get(\"agent_id\")\n53\t        if not isinstance(agent_id, str) or not agent_id:\n54\t            agent_id = agent_name or \"unknown\"\n55\t\n56\t        try:\n57\t            report_state.record_sdk_usage(\n58\t                agent_id=agent_id,\n59\t                agent_name=agent_name,\n60\t                model=self._model,\n61\t                usage=response.usage,\n62\t            )\n63\t        except Exception:\n64\t            logger.exception(\"failed to record SDK usage for agent %s\", agent_id)\n65\t\n66\t        if self._max_budget_usd is not None:\n67\t            cost = report_state.get_total_llm_cost()\n68\t            if cost >= self._max_budget_usd:\n69\t                raise BudgetExceededError(\n70\t                    f\"Token budget of ${self._max_budget_usd:.2f} exceeded (spent ${cost:.4f})\"\n71\t                )\n72\t"}]},"uuid":"6d2470b6-7767-4b22-93ab-b37abb18bc77","timestamp":"2026-07-20T01:21:02.818Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a4804a0d-3aa7-4adc-a981-9124cd0e43a2","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

\"\").strip().lower()\n29\t    for prefix in (\"litellm/\", \"any-llm/\"):\n30\t        if name.startswith(prefix):\n31\t            name = name[len(prefix) :]\n32\t            break\n33\t    return name.startswith(\"openai/\") or is_known_openai_bare_model(name)\n34\t\n35\t\n36\tdef build_root_task(scan_config: dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n37\t    targets = scan_config.get(\"targets\", []) or []\n38\t    diff_scope = scan_config.get(\"diff_scope\") or {}\n39\t    user_instructions = scan_config.get(\"user_instructions\", \"\") or \"\"\n40\t\n41\t    sections: dict[str, list[str]] = {\n42\t target.get(\"type\")\n50\t        details = target.get(\"details\") or {}\n51\t        workspace_subdir = details.get(\"workspace_subdir\")\n52\t        workspace_path = f\"/workspace/{workspace_subdir}\" if workspace_subdir else \"/workspace\"\n53\t\n54\t        if ttype == \"repository\":\n55\t            url = details.get(\"target_repo\", \"\")\n56\t            cloned = details.get(\"cloned_repo_path\")\n57\t            sections[\"Repositories\"].append(\n58\t                f\"- {url} (available at: {workspace_path})\" if cloned else f\"- {url}\",\n59\t            )\n60\t        elif ttype == \"local_code\":\n61\t            path = details.get(\"target_path\", \"unknown\")\n62\t            suffix = \", read-only mount\" if details.get(\"mount\") else \"\"\n63\t            sections[\"Local Codebases\"].append(f\"- {path} (available at: {workspace_path}{suffix})\")\n64\t        elif ttype == \"web_application\":\n65\t            sections[\"URLs\"].append(f\"- {details.get('target_url', '')}\")\n66\t        elif ttype == \"ip_address\":\n67\t            sections[\"IP Addresses\"].append(f\"- {details.get('target_ip', '')}\")\n68\t\n69\t    parts: list[str] = []\n70\t    for label, items in sections.items():\n71\t        if items:\n72\t            parts.append(f\"\\n\\n{label}:\")\n73\t Whatever the caller asked for, in order.\n32\t    2. ``scan_modes/<mode>`` (always).\n33\t    3. ``tooling/agent_browser`` (always — every agent has shell + the\n34\t       agent-browser CLI).\n35\t    4. ``tooling/python`` (always — Python runs through ``exec_command``;\n36\t       sandbox scripts can import ``caido_api`` for Caido automation).\n37\t    5. ``coordination/root_agent`` for the root agent only — orchestration\n38\t       guidance for delegating to specialist subagents.\n39\t    6. Whitebox-specific skills if applicable.\n40\t    \"\"\"\n41\t    ordered: list[str] = list(requested or [])\n42\t    ordered.append(f\"scan_modes/{scan_mode}\")\n43\t    ordered.append(\"tooling/agent_browser\")\n44\t    ordered.append(\"tooling/python\")\n45\t    if is_root:\n46\t        ordered.append(\"coordination/root_agent\")\n47\t    if is_whitebox:\n48\t        ordered.append(\"coordination/source_aware_whitebox\")\n49\t        ordered.append(\"custom/source_aware_sast\")\n50\t\n51\t    deduped: list[str] = []\n52\t    seen: set[str] = set()\n53\t    for skill in ordered:\n54\t        if skill and skill not in seen:\n55\t            deduped.append(skill)\n56\t            seen.add(skill)\n57\t    return deduped\n58\t\n59\t\n60\tdef render_system_prompt(\n61\t    *,\n62\t    skills: list[str] | None = None,\n63\t    scan_mode: str = \"deep\",\n64\t    is_whitebox: bool = False,\n65\t    is_root: bool = False,\n66\t    interactive: bool = False,\n67\t    system_prompt_context: dict[str, Any] | None = None,\n68\t) -> str:\n69\t    \"\"\"Render the system prompt. Returns empty string on template failure.\"\"\"\n70\t    try:\n71\t        prompt_dir = get_strix_resource_path(\"agents\", _PROMPT_DIRNAME)\n72\t        loader_dirs = [prompt_dir, *skill_search_dirs()]\n73\t        env = Environment(\n74\t            loader=FileSystemLoader(loader_dirs),\n75\t            autoescape=select_autoescape(\n76\t                enabled_extensions=(),\n77\t                default_for_string=False,\n78\t            ),\n79\t        )\n80\t\n81\t        skills_to_load = _resolve_skills(\n82\t            requested=skills,\n83\t            scan_mode=scan_mode,\n84\t            is_whitebox=is_whitebox,\n85\t            is_root=is_root,\n86\t        )\n87\t        skill_content = load_skills(skills_to_load)\n88\t        env.globals[\"get_skill\"] = lambda name: skill_content.get(name, \"\")\n89\t\n90\t        rendered = env.get_template(\"system_prompt.jinja\").render(\n91\t            loaded_skill_names=list(skill_content.keys()),\n92\t            available_skills=get_available_skills(),\n93\t            interactive=interactive,\n94\t            system_prompt_context=system_prompt_context or {},\n95\t            **skill_content,\n96\t        )\n97\t    except Exception:\n98\t        logger.exception(\"render_system_prompt failed; returning empty prompt\")\n99\t        return \"\"\n100\t    else:\n101\t        logger.debug(\n102\t            \"render_system_prompt: scan_mode=%s root=%s whitebox=%s skills=%d prompt_len=%d\",\n103\t            scan_mode,\n104\t            is_root,\n105\t            is_whitebox,\n106\t            len(skill_content),\n107\t            len(rendered),\n108\t        )\n109\t        return str(rendered)\n110\t"}]},"uuid":"c7f6e71d-4587-4fa9-be8f-3fd2e94c57b3","timestamp":"2026-07-20T01:21:10.853Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5116a387-7377-47f1-9725-56160abef3ec","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"3dc0cda9-637e-4e08-877f-95fc36ec1a91","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"3bfd3d93-3d8c-4d5a-8244-149007afff7e","agentId":"ae42573080b47b5cd","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016hSRwyCTGSbkWhxXyQ77d4","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Multi-agent graph tools backed by AgentCoordinator.\"\"\"\n2\t\n3\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n4\t\n5\timport asyncio\n6\timport json\n7\timport logging\n8\timport uuid\n9\tfrom collections import Counter\n10\tfrom datetime import UTC, datetime\n11\tfrom typing import Any, Literal, get_args\n12\t\n13\tfrom agents import RunContextWrapper, function_tool\n14\t\n15\tfrom strix.core.agents import Status, coordinator_from_context\n16\tfrom strix.skills import validate_requested_skills\n17\t\n18\t\n19\t_ACTIVE_STATUSES: frozenset[str] = frozenset({\"running\", \"waiting\"})\n20\t\n21\t\n22\tlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n23\t\n24\t\n25\tdef _ctx(ctx: RunContextWrapper) -> dict[str, Any]:\n26\t    return ctx.context if isinstance(ctx.context, dict) else {}\n27\t\n28\t\n29\tdef _render_completion_report(\n30\t    *,\n31\t    agent_name: str,\n32\t    agent_id: str,\n33\t    task: str,\n34\t    success: bool,\n35\t    result_summary: str,\n36\t    findings: list[str],\n37\t    recommendations: list[str],\n38\t) -> str:\n39\t    \"\"\"Render a child's completion report as plain structured text.\n40\t\n41\t    Goes into the parent's SDK session with coordinator-added sender\n42\t    metadata, so this body just carries the contents. No XML — no\n43\t    escaping concerns, no parser ambiguity.\n44\t    \"\"\"\n45\t    status = \"SUCCESS\" if success else \"FAILED\"\n46\t    completion_time = datetime.now(UTC).isoformat()\n47\t\n48\t    lines: list[str] = [\n49\t        f\"== Completion report from {agent_name} ({agent_id}) ==\",\n50\t        f\"Status: {status}\",\n51\t        f\"Time: {completion_time}\",\n52\t    ]\n53\t    if task:\n54\t        lines.append(f\"Task: {task}\")\n55\t    lines.append(\"\")\n56\t    lines.append(\"Summary:\")\n57\t    lines.append(result_summary or \"(none)\")\n58\t    if findings:\n59\t        lines.append(\"\")\n60\t        lines.append(\"Findings:\")\n61\t        lines.extend(f\"- {f}\" for f in findings)\n62\t    if recommendations:\n63\t        lines.append(\"\")\n64\t        lines.append(\"Recommendations:\")\n65\t        lines.extend(f\"- {r}\" for r in recommendations)\n66\t    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n67\t\n68\t\n69\t@function_tool(timeout=30)\n70\tasync def view_agent_graph(ctx: RunContextWrapper) -> str:\n71\t    \"\"\"Print the multi-agent tree — every agent, its parent, its status.\n72\t\n73\t    Use before spawning a new agent (don't duplicate work — check whether\n74\t    something specialized for that task already exists) and any time you\n75\t    want a snapshot of who's still ``running`` / ``waiting`` /\n76\t    ``completed`` / ``crashed`` / ``stopped``. Output is an indented\n77\t    bullet list with status in brackets; the agent that called this tool\n78\t    is marked ``← you``.\n79\t    \"\"\"\n80\t    inner = _ctx(ctx)\n81\t    coordinator = coordinator_from_context(inner)\n82\t    me = inner.get(\"agent_id\")\n83\t    if coordinator is None:\n84\t        return json.dumps(\n85\t            {\"success\": False, \"error\": \"Agent coordinator not initialized in context\"},\n86\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n87\t            default=str,\n88\t        )\n89\t\n90\t    parent_of, statuses, names = await coordinator.graph_snapshot()\n91\t\n92\t    lines: list[str] = []\n93\t\n94\t    def render(aid: str, depth: int) -> None:\n95\t        status = statuses.get(aid, \"?\")\n96\t        marker = \"  ← you\" if aid == me else \"\"\n97\t        lines.append(f\"{'  ' * depth}- {names.get(aid, aid)} ({aid}) [{status}]{marker}\")\n98\t        for child, p in parent_of.items():\n99\t            if p == aid:\n100\t                render(child, depth + 1)\n101\t\n102\t    roots = [aid for aid, parent in parent_of.items() if parent is None]\n103\t    for root in roots:\n104\t        render(root, 0)\n105\t\n106\t    counts = Counter(statuses.values())\n107\t    summary: dict[str, int] = {\"total\": len(parent_of)}\n108\t    for status_name in get_args(Status):\n109\t        summary[status_name] = counts.get(status_name, 0)\n110\t    return json.dumps(\n111\t        {\n112\t            \"success\": True,\n113\t            \"graph_structure\": \"\\n\".join(lines) or \"(no agents)\",\n114\t            \"summary\": summary,\n115\t        },\n116\t        ensure_ascii=False,\n117\t        default=str,\n118\t    )\n119\t\n120\t\n121\t@function_tool(timeout=30)\n122\tasync def send_message_to_agent(\n123\t    ctx: RunContextWrapper,\n124\t    target_agent_id: str,\n125\t    message: str,\n126\t    message_type: Literal[\"query\", \"instruction\", \"information\"] = \"information\",\n127\t    priority: Literal[\"low\", \"normal\", \"high\", \"urgent\"] = \"normal\",\n128\t) -> str:\n129\t    \"\"\"Send a message to another agent's inbox — sparingly.\n130\t\n131\t    Inter-agent messages are appended to the target's SDK session and\n132\t    interrupt any active target turn so the next run cycle sees them.\n133\t    Use only when essential:\n134\t\n135\t    - Sharing a discovered finding/credential another agent needs.\n136\t    - Asking a specialist a focused question.\n137\t    - Coordinating who covers what (avoid overlap).\n138\t    - Telling a child to wrap up or change course.\n139\t\n140\t    **Don't** use for routine \"hello/status\" pings, for context the\n141\t    target already has (children inherit parent history), or when\n142\t    parent/child completion via ``agent_finish`` already covers the\n143\t    flow. Messages to any registered agent wake it, regardless of\n144\t    status, so a follow-up can restart a completed/stopped/failed agent.\n145\t\n146\t    Args:\n147\t        target_agent_id: Recipient's 8-char id.\n148\t        message: The full message body. Be specific — include payloads,\n149\t            URLs, or what you want them to do, not just headlines.\n150\t        message_type: ``query`` (you want a reply), ``instruction``\n151\t            (you're directing them), ``information`` (FYI, no reply\n152\t            expected). Default ``information``.\n153\t        priority: ``low`` / ``normal`` / ``high`` / ``urgent``.\n154\t    \"\"\"\n155\t    inner = _ctx(ctx)\n156\t    coordinator = coordinator_from_context(inner)\n157\t    me = inner.get(\"agent_id\")\n158\t    if coordinator is None or me is None:\n159\t        return json.dumps(\n160\t            {\"success\": False, payload\n219\t\n220\t\n221\t@function_tool(timeout=601)\n222\tasync def wait_for_message(  # noqa: PLR0911\n223\t    ctx: RunContextWrapper,\n224\t    reason: str = \"Waiting for messages from other agents\",\n225\t    timeout_seconds: int = 600,\n226\t) -> str:\n227\t    \"\"\"Pause this agent until a message lands in its inbox (or timeout).\n228\t\n229\t    Use when you have nothing useful to do until a child/peer responds\n230\t    — typically after spawning subagents and you want to wait for\n231\t    their completion reports. The agent automatically resumes when any\n232\t    message arrives, so pick a ``timeout_seconds`` proportional to the\n233\t    work you're awaiting.\n234\t\n235\t    **Critical caveats:**\n236\t\n237\t    - **Never** call this if you finished your own task and have **no**\n238\t      child agents running — that's a permanent stall. Call\n239\t      ``finish_scan`` (root) or ``agent_finish`` (subagent) instead.\n240\t    - If you're waiting on an agent that **isn't your child**, message\n241\t      it first asking it to ping you when done — otherwise it has no\n242\t      reason to send to your inbox and you'll wait the full timeout.\n243\t    - Children update the parent automatically via ``agent_finish``\n244\t      → no extra coordination needed.\n245\t\n246\t    Args:\n247\t        reason: One-line note shown in graph snapshots while you're\n248\t            waiting (helps a human or sibling agent debug who's stuck\n249\t            on what).\n250\t        timeout_seconds: Max seconds to wait (default 600). This is only\n251\t            a cap — the tool returns the INSTANT a message arrives, so a\n252\t            larger value never makes you wait longer when the reply does\n253\t            come. Right-size it to what you're waiting on: a short wait\n254\t            (e.g. 10-60s) for a quick ack or a small/fast subtask, and a\n255\t            longer one (e.g. ~100-200s) only for genuinely long-running\n256\t            work (deep recon, exploitation, a full sub-scan). The cap only\n257\t            bites when the expected message never arrives — so an oversized\n258\t            timeout on a trivial wait just strands you idle until it\n259\t            elapses. On timeout the tool returns and you decide whether to\n260\t            keep working or wait again. (Applies to autonomous multi-agent\n261\t            runs; in interactive/chat sessions the agent instead parks until\n262\t            a message arrives and this cap is not enforced.)\n263\t    \"\"\"\n264\t    inner = _ctx(ctx)\n265\t    coordinator = coordinator_from_context(inner)\n266\t    me = inner.get(\"agent_id\")\n267\t    interactive = bool(inner.get(\"interactive\", False))\n268\t    if coordinator is None or me is None:\n269\t        return json.dumps(\n270\t            {\"success\": False, \"error\": \"Agent coordinator or agent_id missing in context\"},\n271\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n272\t            default=str,\n273\t        )\n274\t\n275\t    async with coordinator._lock:\n276\t        stopped = coordinator.statuses.get(me) == \"stopped\"\n277\t    if stopped:\n278\t        return json.dumps(\n279\t            {\n280\t                \"success\": True,\n281\t                \"wait_outcome\": \"stopped\",\n282\t                \"reason\": reason,\n283\t                \"note\": \"Wait ended because this agent is stopped.\",\n284\t            },\n285\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n286\t            default=str,\n287\t json.dumps(\n293\t            {\n294\t                \"success\": True,\n295\t                \"wait_outcome\": \"message_arrived\",\n296\t                \"pending_messages\": pending,\n297\t coordinator.consume_pending(me, include_items=True)\n349\t    await coordinator.mark_running(me)\n350\t\n351\t    return json.dumps(\n352\t        {\n353\t            \"success\": True,\n354\t      },\n359\t        ensure_ascii=False,\n360\t        default=str,\n361\t    )\n362\t\n363\t\n364\t@function_tool(timeout=120)\n365\tasync def create_agent(\n366\t    ctx: RunContextWrapper,\n367\t Five skills = upper\n390\t      bound.\n391\t    - Match the ``name`` to the focus (``XSS Specialist``,\n392\t      ``SQLi Validator``, ``Auth Specialist``).\n393\t\n394\t    **When to spawn vs do it yourself:**\n395\t\n396\t    - Spawn when the subtask is large, parallelizable, or needs\n397\t      different specialization than what you're already doing.\n398\t    - Don't spawn for trivial one-shot probes — just run the tool\n399\t      yourself.\n400\t\n401\t    Args:\n402\t        name: Human-readable child name (used in graph views and\n403\t            ``send_message_to_agent`` flows).\n404\t        task: Specific objective. ``[\"xss\", \"sql_injection\"]``).\n410\t            Max 5; prefer 1-3.\n411\t    \"\"\"\n412\t    inner = _ctx(ctx)\n413\t    coordinator = coordinator_from_context(inner)\n414\t    parent_id = inner.get(\"agent_id\")\n415\t    spawner = inner.get(\"spawn_child_agent\")\n416\t\n417\t    if coordinator is None or parent_id is None:\n418\t        return json.dumps(\n419\t callable(spawner):\n424\t        return json.dumps(\n425\t            {\n426\t                \"success\": False,\n427\t                \"error\": \"Scan runner did not provide a child-agent spawner in context\",\n428\t            },\n429\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n430\t            default=str,\n431\t        )\n432\t\n433\t    skill_list = list(skills or [])\n434\t    skill_error = validate_requested_skills(skill_list)\n435\t    if skill_error:\n436\t        return json.dumps(\n437\t            {\"success\": False, \"error\": skill_error, \"agent_id\": None},\n438\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n439\t            default=str,\n440\t        )\n441\t\n442\t    parent_history = list(ctx.turn_input) if inherit_context and ctx.turn_input else []\n443\t    try:\n444\t        result = await spawner(\n445\t            parent_ctx=inner,\n446\t            name=name,\n447\t            task=task,\n448\t            skills=skill_list,\n449\t            parent_history=parent_history,\n450\t        )\n451\t    except Exception as e:\n452\t        logger.exception(\"create_agent: scan runner failed to spawn child '%s'\", name)\n453\t        return json.dumps(\n454\t            {\"success\": False, \"error\": f\"child spawn failed: {e!s}\"},\n455\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n456\t            default=str,\n457\t        )\n458\t\n459\t )\n473\t\n474\t\n475\t@function_tool(timeout=30)\n476\tasync def agent_finish(\n477\t    ctx: RunContextWrapper,\n478\t    result_summary: str,\n479\t    findings: list[str] | None = None,\n480\t Posts a structured completion report to the parent's inbox\n491\t       (when ``report_to_parent`` is true).\n492\t    3. Stops this subagent's execution.\n493\t\n494\t    **Vulnerability findings must already be filed via\n495\t    ``create_vulnerability_report`` (or ``create_dependency_report``\n496\t    for known-CVE dependency/supply-chain findings) before calling\n497\t    this.** The ``findings`` field here is for narrative summary only\n498\t    — it does not register vulns in the scan report.\n499\t\n500\t    Write the summary as if the parent has no idea what you were\n501\t    doing: what did you test, what did you find/confirm/rule out,\n502\t    what's still open.\n503\t\n504\t    Args:\n505\t        result_summary: What you accomplished and discovered. Concrete\n506\t            and specific (URLs, parameters, payloads that worked).\n507\t        findings: Optional bullet list of confirmed observations. For\n508\t            credit-bearing vulnerabilities, file\n509\t            ``create_vulnerability_report`` first (or\n510\t            ``create_dependency_report`` for dependency CVEs); this is\n511\t            for narrative.\n512\t        success: Whether the assigned subtask was completed\n513\t            successfully. Default ``True``.\n516\t        final_recommendations: Optional next-step suggestions for the\n517\t            parent (e.g., \"prioritize testing X\", \"spawn an agent to\n518\t            cover Y\").\n519\t    \"\"\"\n520\t    inner = _ctx(ctx)\n521\t    coordinator = coordinator_from_context(inner)\n522\t    me = inner.get(\"agent_id\")\n523\t    if coordinator is None or me is None:\n524\t        return json.dumps(\n525\t            {\"success\": False, \"error\": \"Agent coordinator or agent_id missing in context\"},\n526\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n527\t            default=str,\n528\t        )\n529\t\n530\t    parent_id = inner.get(\"parent_id\")\n531\t    if parent_id is None:\n532\t        return json.dumps(\n533\t            {\n534\t                \"success\": False,\n535\t                \"error\": (\n536\t                    \"agent_finish is for subagents. Root/main agents must call finish_scan instead\"\n537\t                ),\n538\t            },\n539\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n540\t            default=str,\n541\t        )\n542\t\n543\t    parent_notified = False\n544\t    if report_to_parent:\n545\t        async with coordinator._lock:\n546\t            agent_name = coordinator.names.get(me, me)\n547\t        report = _render_completion_report(\n548\t            agent_name=agent_name,\n549\t            agent_id=me,\n550\t            task=str(inner.get(\"task\", \"\")),\n551\t            success=success,\n552\t coordinator.send(\n557\t            parent_id,\n558\t            {\n559\t                \"id\": f\"report_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}\",\n560\t                \"from\": me,\n561\t True\n567\t\n568\t    logger.info(\n569\t        \"agent_finish: %s success=%s findings=%d parent_notified=%s\",\n570\t        me,\n571\t        success,\n572\t        len(findings or []),\n573\t \"\",\n598\t) -> str:\n599\t    \"\"\"Gracefully stop a running agent (and optionally its descendants).\n600\t\n601\t    Uses the SDK's ``RunResultStreaming.cancel(mode=\"after_turn\")`` so the\n602\t    target's current turn finishes — including saving items to its\n603\t    session — before the run loop honors the cancel. The agent's\n604\t    interactive outer loop parks as ``stopped``; later user/peer\n605\t    messages can wake it again.\n606\t\n607\t    Use sparingly. Prefer ``send_message_to_agent`` (asking the agent\n608\t    to wrap up) for soft-stop scenarios. Reach for ``stop_agent`` when\n609\t    a child has gone off-track and won't self-correct.\n610\t\n611\t    Args:\n612\t        target_agent_id: The 8-char id from ``view_agent_graph`` /\n613\t            ``create_agent``. Cannot stop yourself.\n614\t        cascade: If ``True`` (default), also stop every descendant of\n615\t            ``target_agent_id`` leaves-first. ``False`` stops only the\n616\t            target.\n617\t        reason: Optional human-readable reason for the stop, surfaced\n618\t            in logs and telemetry.\n619\t    \"\"\"\n620\t    inner = _ctx(ctx)\n621\t    coordinator = coordinator_from_context(inner)\n622\t    me = inner.get(\"agent_id\")\n623\t    if coordinator is None or me is None:\n624\t        return json.dumps(\n625\t            {\"success\": False, \"error\": \"Agent coordinator or agent_id missing in context\"},\n626\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n627\t            default=str,\n628\t        )\n629\t    if target_agent_id == me:\n630\t        return json.dumps(\n631\t            {\n632\t                \"success\": False,\n633\t                \"error\": \"Cannot stop yourself; call agent_finish or finish_scan instead\",\n634\t            },\n635\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n636\t            default=str,\n637\t        )\n638\t    _, statuses, _ = await coordinator.graph_snapshot()\n639\t    if target_agent_id not in statuses:\n640\t        return json.dumps(\n641\t            {\"success\": False, \"error\": f\"Unknown agent_id: {target_agent_id}\"},\n642\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n643\t            default=str,\n644\t        )\n645\t\n646\t    current_status = statuses[target_agent_id]\n647\t    if current_status not in _ACTIVE_STATUSES:\n648\t        return json.dumps(\n649\t            {\n650\t                \"success\": False,\n651\t                \"error\": (\n652\t                    f\"Agent {target_agent_id} is already '{current_status}'; \"\n653\t                    \"stop_agent only acts on running/waiting agents — use \"\n654\t                    \"view_agent_graph to find still-active descendants and \"\n655\t                    \"stop them individually, or send_message_to_agent if you \"\n656\t                    \"want to wake this one with new instructions\"\n657\t                ),\n658\t                \"target_agent_id\": target_agent_id,\n659\t                \"current_status\": current_status,\n660\t            },\n661\t            ensure_ascii=False,\n662\t            default=str,\n663\t        )\n664\t\n665\t    if cascade:\n666\t        await coordinator.cancel_descendants_graceful(target_agent_id)\n667\t    else:\n668\t        await coordinator.request_stop(target_agent_id)\n669\t\n670\t    logger.info(\n671\t        \"stop_agent: target=%s cascade=%s reason=%r\",\n672\t        target_agent_id,\n673\t        cascade,\n674\t        reason,\n675\t    )\n676\t    return json.dumps(\n677\t        {\n678\t            \"success\": True,\n679\t            \"target_agent_id\": target_agent_id,\n680\t            \"cascade\": cascade,\n681\t            \"reason\": reason,\n682\t            \"note\": \"Cancellation is graceful — current turn completes first.\",\n683\t        },\n684\t        ensure_ascii=False,\n685\t        default=str,\n686\t    )\n687\t"}]},"uuid":"4872ca63-18a7-40d8-8ab2-9e4c13a5bd6a","timestamp":"2026-07-20T01:21:11.701Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3dc0cda9-637e-4e08-877f-95fc36ec1a91","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

We subclass\n6\tand reimplement the method body verbatim from the SDK source, with three\n7\tdeltas:\n8\t\n9\t1. Drop the SDK's ``entrypoint=[\"tail\"]`` override; supply ``[\"tail\", \"-f\",\n10\t   \"/dev/null\"]`` as ``command`` instead. This lets our image's\n11\t   ``docker-entrypoint.sh`` actually run — without it, ``caido-cli`` never\n12\t   starts inside the container and ``bootstrap_caido`` retries against a\n13\t   dead port.\n14\t2. Append NET_ADMIN/NET_RAW to ``cap_add`` (required by ``nmap -sS`` and\n15\t   other raw-socket tools).\n16\t3. Add ``host.docker.internal`` → host-gateway to ``extra_hosts`` so the\n17\t   agent can reach host-served apps.\n18\t\n19\tPinned to ``openai-agents==0.14.6``. Bumping the SDK requires\n20\tre-merging the parent body. Track upstream for an injection hook.\n21\t\"\"\"\n22\t\n23\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n24\t\n25\timport contextlib\n26\timport logging\n27\timport os\n28\timport uuid\n29\tfrom typing import Any, cast\n30\t\n31\tfrom agents.sandbox.errors import ExposedPortUnavailableError\n32\tfrom agents.sandbox.manifest import Manifest\n33\tfrom agents.sandbox.sandboxes.docker import (\n34\t    DockerSandboxClient,\n35\t    DockerSandboxSession,\n36\t    _build_docker_volume_mounts,\n37\t    _docker_port_key,\n38\t    _manifest_requires_fuse,\n39\t    _manifest_requires_sys_admin,\n40\t)\n41\tfrom agents.sandbox.session.sandbox_session import SandboxSession\n42\tfrom agents.sandbox.types import ExposedPortEndpoint\n43\tfrom docker import errors as docker_errors  # type: ignore[import-untyped, unused-ignore]\n44\tfrom docker.models.containers import Container  # type: ignore[import-untyped, unused-ignore]\n45\tfrom docker.types import LogConfig  # type: ignore[import-untyped, unused-ignore]\n46\tfrom docker.types import Mount as DockerSDKMount  # type: ignore[import-untyped, unused-ignore]\n47\tfrom docker.utils import parse_repository_tag  # type: ignore[import-untyped, unused-ignore]\n48\tfrom requests.exceptions import RequestException\n49\t\n50\t\n51\tlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n52\t\n53\t\n54\t_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENV = \"STRIX_DOCKER_SANDBOX_NETWORK\"\n55\t\n56\t\n57\tdef _sandbox_network() -> str | None:\n58\t    value = os.environ.get(_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENV, \"\").strip()\n59\t    return value or None\n60\t\n61\t\n62\tdef _apply_sandbox_network(create_kwargs: dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n63\t    network = _sandbox_network()\n64\t    if network:\n65\t        create_kwargs[\"network\"] = network\n66\t        create_kwargs.pop(\"ports\", None)\n67\t\n68\t\n69\tdef _apply_resource_limits(create_kwargs: dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n70\t    \"\"\"Apply optional cgroup resource caps from the environment. Unset/blank\n71\t    values leave docker's default (unbounded), so this is opt-in per host.\"\"\"\n72\t    mem_limit = os.environ.get(\"STRIX_SANDBOX_MEM_LIMIT\", \"\").strip()\n73\t    if mem_limit:\n74\t        create_kwargs[\"mem_limit\"] = mem_limit\n75\t\n76\t    shm_size = os.environ.get(\"STRIX_SANDBOX_SHM_SIZE\", \"\").strip()\n77\t    if shm_size:\n78\t        create_kwargs[\"shm_size\"] = shm_size\n79\t\n80\t    cpus = os.environ.get(\"STRIX_SANDBOX_CPUS\", \"\").strip()\n81\t    if cpus:\n82\t        with contextlib.suppress(ValueError, OverflowError):\n83\t            nano_cpus = int(float(cpus) * 1_000_000_000)\n84\t            if 0 < nano_cpus <= 2**63 - 1:\n85\t                create_kwargs[\"nano_cpus\"] = nano_cpus\n86\t\n87\t    pids_limit = os.environ.get(\"STRIX_SANDBOX_PIDS_LIMIT\", \"\").strip()\n88\t    if pids_limit:\n89\t        with contextlib.suppress(ValueError):\n90\t            create_kwargs[\"pids_limit\"] = int(pids_limit)\n91\t\n92\t\n93\tdef _apply_log_limits(create_kwargs: dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n94\t    \"\"\"Bound the container's json-file log so a runaway process in the sandbox\n95\t    (e.g. a tool that busy-loops writing to stdout) cannot fill the host disk\n96\t    and take the Docker daemon down with it.\n97\t\n98\t    Unlike the cgroup caps above, this defaults **on** — docker's own default\n99\t    is an unbounded json-file, which is unsafe for an autonomous agent that\n100\t    executes arbitrary commands. ``max-file`` rotation means the on-disk cap is\n101\t    ``max-size * max-file``. Set ``STRIX_SANDBOX_LOG_MAX_SIZE`` to ``0``/``off``\n102\t    to opt back out to docker's default.\"\"\"\n103\t    max_size = os.environ.get(\"STRIX_SANDBOX_LOG_MAX_SIZE\", \"50m\").strip()\n104\t    if max_size.lower() in (\"0\", \"off\", \"none\", \"unlimited\"):\n105\t        return\n106\t    max_file = os.environ.get(\"STRIX_SANDBOX_LOG_MAX_FILE\", \"3\").strip() or \"3\"\n107\t    create_kwargs[\"log_config\"] = LogConfig(\n108\t        type=LogConfig.types.JSON,\n109\t        config={\"max-size\": max_size, \"max-file\": max_file},\n110\t    )\n111\t\n112\t\n113\tclass StrixDockerSandboxSession(DockerSandboxSession):\n114\t    sandbox_network: str = \"\"\n115\t\n116\t    async def _resolve_exposed_port(self, port: int) -> ExposedPortEndpoint:\n117\t        try:\n118\t            self._container.reload()\n119\t        except docker_errors.APIError as e:\n120\t            raise ExposedPortUnavailableError(\n121\t                port=port,\n122\t                exposed_ports=self.state.exposed_ports,\n123\t                reason=\"backend_unavailable\",\n124\t                context={\n125\t                    \"backend\": \"docker\",\n126\t                    \"detail\": \"container_reload_failed\",\n127\t                    \"network\": self.sandbox_network,\n128\t                },\n129\t                cause=e,\n130\t            ) from e\n131\t\n132\t        attrs = getattr(self._container, \"attrs\", {}) or {}\n133\t        networks = attrs.get(\"NetworkSettings\", {}).get(\"Networks\", {})\n134\t        endpoint = networks.get(self.sandbox_network) or {}\n135\t        ip = endpoint.get(\"IPAddress\") or endpoint.get(\"GlobalIPv6Address\")\n136\t        if not isinstance(ip, str) or not ip:\n137\t            raise ExposedPortUnavailableError(\n138\t                port=port,\n139\t                exposed_ports=self.state.exposed_ports,\n140\t                reason=\"backend_unavailable\",\n141\t                context={\n142\t                    \"backend\": \"docker\",\n143\t                    \"detail\": \"container_not_on_network\",\n144\t                    \"network\": self.sandbox_network,\n145\t                },\n146\t            )\n147\t        host = f\"[{ip}]\" if \":\" in ip else ip\n148\t        return ExposedPortEndpoint(host=host, port=port, tls=False)\n149\t\n150\t\n151\tclass StrixDockerSandboxClient(DockerSandboxClient):\n152\t    # Host directories to bind-mount into the container, set by the docker\n153\t    # backend before ``create()``. Each item is ``{source, target, read_only}``.\n154\t    strix_bind_mounts: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None\n155\t\n156\t    async def _create_container(\n157\t        self,\n158\t        image: str,\n159\t        *,\n160\t        manifest: Manifest | None = None,\n161\t        exposed_ports: tuple[int, ...] = (),\n162\t        session_id: uuid.UUID | None = None,\n163\t    ) -> Container:\n164\t        # ----- BEGIN VERBATIM COPY of DockerSandboxClient._create_container -----\n165\t        # SDK ref: src/agents/sandbox/sandboxes/docker.py:1434-1477 (v0.14.6).\n166\t        if not self.image_exists(image):\n167\t            repo, tag = parse_repository_tag(image)\n168\t            self.docker_client.images.pull(repo, tag=tag or None, all_tags=False)\n169\t\n170\t        assert self.image_exists(image)\n171\t        environment: dict[str, str] | None = None\n172\t        if manifest:\n173\t            environment = await manifest.environment.resolve()\n174\t        # Strix delta from the SDK body: drop ``entrypoint`` override and\n175\t        # supply ``tail -f /dev/null`` as ``command`` so the image's\n176\t        # ENTRYPOINT (``docker-entrypoint.sh``) runs setup, then ``exec\n177\t        # \"$@\"`` becomes ``exec tail -f /dev/null`` for the keep-alive.\n178\t        # Without this, caido-cli + the in-container CA trust never get\n179\t        # initialized.\n180\t        create_kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {\n181\t            \"image\": image,\n182\t            \"detach\": True,\n183\t            \"command\": [\"tail\", \"-f\", \"/dev/null\"],\n184\t            \"environment\": environment,\n185\t        }\n186\t        if manifest is not None:\n187\t            docker_mounts = _build_docker_volume_mounts(\n188\t                manifest,\n189\t                session_id=session_id,\n190\t            )\n191\t            if docker_mounts:\n192\t                create_kwargs[\"mounts\"] = docker_mounts\n193\t            if _manifest_requires_fuse(manifest):\n194\t                create_kwargs.update(\n195\t                    devices=[\"/dev/fuse\"],\n196\t                    cap_add=[\"SYS_ADMIN\"],\n197\t                    security_opt=[\"apparmor:unconfined\"],\n198\t                )\n199\t            elif _manifest_requires_sys_admin(manifest):\n200\t                create_kwargs.update(\n201\t                    cap_add=[\"SYS_ADMIN\"],\n202\t                    security_opt=[\"apparmor:unconfined\"],\n203\t                )\n204\t        if exposed_ports:\n205\t            create_kwargs[\"ports\"] = {\n206\t                _docker_port_key(port): (\"127.0.0.1\", None) for port in exposed_ports\n207\t            }\n208\t        # ----- END VERBATIM COPY -----\n209\t\n210\t        # Strix injections — append, don't overwrite, so FUSE/SYS_ADMIN survives.\n211\t        cap_add = create_kwargs.setdefault(\"cap_add\", [])\n212\t        if not isinstance(cap_add, list):\n213\t            cap_add = list(cap_add)\n214\t            create_kwargs[\"cap_add\"] = cap_add\n215\t        for cap in (\"NET_ADMIN\", \"NET_RAW\"):\n216\t            if cap not in cap_add:\n217\t                cap_add.append(cap)\n218\t\n219\t        extra_hosts = create_kwargs.setdefault(\"extra_hosts\", {})\n220\t        extra_hosts[\"host.docker.internal\"] = \"host-gateway\"\n221\t\n222\t        _apply_sandbox_network(create_kwargs)\n223\t        _apply_resource_limits(create_kwargs)\n224\t        _apply_log_limits(create_kwargs)\n225\t\n226\t        # Strix injection: host bind mounts (e.g. large repos passed via --mount)\n227\t        # that bypass the SDK's file-by-file LocalDir copy.\n228\t        bind_mounts = getattr(self, \"strix_bind_mounts\", ())\n229\t        if bind_mounts:\n230\t            mounts = create_kwargs.setdefault(\"mounts\", [])\n231\t            for spec in bind_mounts:\n232\t                mounts.append(\n233\t                    DockerSDKMount(\n234\t                        target=spec[\"target\"],\n235\t                        source=spec[\"source\"],\n236\t                        type=\"bind\",\n237\t                        read_only=spec.get(\"read_only\", True),\n238\t                    )\n239\t                )\n240\t\n241\t        logger.debug(\n242\t            \"Creating sandbox container: image=%s caps=%s exposed_ports=%s\",\n243\t            image,\n244\t            cap_add,\n245\t            list(exposed_ports),\n246\t        )\n247\t        container = self.docker_client.containers.create(**create_kwargs)\n248\t        logger.info(\n249\t            \"Sandbox container created: id=%s image=%s\",\n250\t            container.short_id if hasattr(container, \"short_id\") else \"?\",\n251\t            image,\n252\t        )\n253\t        return container\n254\t\n255\t    async def create(self, **kwargs: Any) -> SandboxSession:\n256\t        session = await super().create(**kwargs)\n257\t        network = _sandbox_network()\n258\t        inner = session._inner\n259\t        if network and isinstance(inner, DockerSandboxSession):\n260\t            inner.__class__ = StrixDockerSandboxSession\n261\t            cast(\"StrixDockerSandboxSession\", inner).sandbox_network = network\n262\t        return session\n263\t\n264\t    async def delete(self, session: SandboxSession) -> SandboxSession:\n265\t        container_id = getattr(getattr(session._inner, \"state\", None), \"container_id\", None)\n266\t        if container_id:\n267\t            # Best-effort kill: NotFound/APIError cover a gone or unhappy\n268\t            # container. RequestException covers a torn-down daemon socket —\n269\t            # containers.get() -> inspect_container raises requests'\n270\t            # ConnectionError, which is a sibling of docker.errors.APIError\n271\t            # under requests.RequestException (not a subclass), so it escapes\n272\t            # an APIError-only suppress and surfaces a full traceback even\n273\t            # though this teardown is meant to be best-effort.\n274\t            with contextlib.suppress(\n275\t                docker_errors.NotFound, docker_errors.APIError, RequestException\n276\t            ):\n277\t                self.docker_client.containers.get(container_id).kill()\n278\t        return await super().delete(session)\n279\t"}]},"uuid":"4c96d3e8-2766-4c56-9727-830dc1cf6054","timestamp":"2026-07-20T01:21:18.865Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d7ceade8-07ec-4af3-be9e-a9d7eed35950","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"4c96d3e8-2766-4c56-9727-830dc1cf6054","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ae42573080b47b5cd","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdCS2dPvfi8UN1my1G5EC","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Nue4h1iUVszufrLVgayMiG","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/tmp/claude-1000/-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main/7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27/scratchpad/strix/strix/runtime/session_manager.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":19044,"cache_read_input_tokens":40305,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":19044,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdCS2bdWUHvU4WZmiyyLd","attributionAgent":"Explore","type":"assistant","uuid":"1b2ecda2-2402-4f7a-ac87-c44e2a2378fa","timestamp":"2026-07-20T01:21:19.838Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"1b2ecda2-2402-4f7a-ac87-c44e2a2378fa","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"3bfd3d93-3d8c-4d5a-8244-149007afff7e","agentId":"ae42573080b47b5cd","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Nue4h1iUVszufrLVgayMiG","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Per-scan sandbox session lifecycle.\"\"\"\n2\t\n3\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n4\t\n5\timport logging\n6\timport shutil\n7\tfrom pathlib import Path\n8\tfrom typing import Any\n9\t\n10\tfrom agents.sandbox.entries import BaseEntry, LocalDir\n11\tfrom agents.sandbox.manifest import Environment, Manifest\n12\t\n13\tfrom strix.config import load_settings\n14\tfrom strix.runtime.backends import get_backend\n15\tfrom strix.runtime.caido_bootstrap import bootstrap_caido\n16\tfrom strix.runtime.local_dir_staging import stage_symlink_safe_dir\n17\t\n18\t\n19\tlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n20\t\n21\t\n22\t# In-container Caido sidecar port (matches the image's caido-cli bind).\n23\t_CONTAINER_CAIDO_PORT = 48080\n24\t\n25\t\n26\t_SESSION_CACHE: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}\n27\t\n28\t# Manifest root inside the container; entry keys hang off this path.\n29\t_WORKSPACE_ROOT = \"/workspace\"\n30\t\n31\t\n32\tdef build_session_entries(\n33\t    local_sources: list[dict[str, Any]],\n34\t) -> tuple[dict[str | Path, BaseEntry], list[dict[str, Any]], list[Path]]:\n35\t    \"\"\"Split local sources into copied manifest entries and host bind mounts.\n36\t\n37\t    Sources flagged ``mount`` are bind-mounted read-only at\n38\t    ``/workspace/<workspace_subdir>`` (not added to the manifest, so the SDK\n39\t    does not stream them in file-by-file). Every other source becomes a\n40\t    ``LocalDir`` entry copied into the container as before. Trees containing\n41\t    symlinks (which the SDK's ``LocalDir`` walker refuses outright) are first\n42\t    staged into a symlink-safe temp copy; those temp dirs are returned so the\n43\t    caller can remove them once the upload completes.\n44\t    \"\"\"\n45\t    entries: dict[str | Path, BaseEntry] = {}\n46\t    bind_mounts: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n47\t    staged_dirs: list[Path] = []\n48\t    for src in local_sources:\n49\t        ws_subdir = src.get(\"workspace_subdir\") or \"\"\n50\t        host_path = src.get(\"source_path\") or \"\"\n51\t        if not ws_subdir or not host_path:\n52\t            continue\n53\t        resolved = Path(host_path).expanduser().resolve()\n54\t        if src.get(\"mount\"):\n55\t            bind_mounts.append(\n56\t                {\n57\t                    \"source\": str(resolved),\n58\t                    \"target\": f\"{_WORKSPACE_ROOT}/{ws_subdir}\",\n59\t                    \"read_only\": True,\n60\t                }\n61\t            )\n62\t        else:\n63\t            upload_path, staged = stage_symlink_safe_dir(resolved)\n64\t            if staged is not None:\n65\t                staged_dirs.append(staged)\n66\t            entries[ws_subdir] = LocalDir(src=upload_path)\n67\t    return entries, bind_mounts, staged_dirs\n68\t\n69\t\n70\tasync def create_or_reuse(\n71\t    scan_id: str,\n72\t    *,\n73\t    image: str,\n74\t    local_sources: list[dict[str, Any]],\n75\t) -> dict[str, Any]:\n76\t    \"\"\"Return the existing session bundle for ``scan_id`` or create a new one.\n77\t\n78\t    Each ``local_sources`` entry exposes its host ``source_path`` at\n79\t    ``/workspace/<workspace_subdir>`` inside the container — copied in, or\n80\t    bind-mounted read-only when the entry is flagged ``mount``.\n81\t    \"\"\"\n82\t    cached = _SESSION_CACHE.get(scan_id)\n83\t    if cached is not None:\n84\t        logger.info(\"Reusing existing sandbox session for scan %s\", scan_id)\n85\t        return cached\n86\t\n87\t    entries, bind_mounts, staged_dirs = build_session_entries(local_sources)\n88\t\n89\t    # Caido runs as an in-container sidecar; HTTP(S) traffic from any\n90\t    # process started via ``session.exec`` (the SDK's Shell tool, etc.)\n91\t    # picks up these env vars automatically. ``NO_PROXY`` keeps the\n92\t    # agent-browser CDP daemon's localhost traffic from looping back\n93\t    # through Caido.\n94\t    container_caido_url = f\"http://127.0.0.1:{_CONTAINER_CAIDO_PORT}\"\n95\t    manifest = Manifest(\n96\t        entries=entries,\n97\t        environment=Environment(\n98\t            value={\n99\t                \"PYTHONUNBUFFERED\": \"1\",\n100\t                \"HOST_GATEWAY\": \"host.docker.internal\",\n101\t                \"http_proxy\": container_caido_url,\n102\t                \"https_proxy\": container_caido_url,\n103\t                \"ALL_PROXY\": container_caido_url,\n104\t                \"NO_PROXY\": \"localhost,127.0.0.1\",\n105\t            },\n106\t        ),\n107\t    )\n108\t\n109\t    backend_name = load_settings().runtime.backend\n110\t    backend = get_backend(backend_name)\n111\t\n112\t    logger.info(\n113\t        \"Creating sandbox session for scan %s (backend=%s, image=%s)\",\n114\t        scan_id,\n115\t        backend_name,\n116\t        image,\n117\t    )\n118\t    try:\n119\t        client, session = await backend(\n120\t            image=image,\n121\t            manifest=manifest,\n122\t            exposed_ports=(_CONTAINER_CAIDO_PORT,),\n123\t            bind_mounts=bind_mounts,\n124\t        )\n125\t    finally:\n126\t        for staged in staged_dirs:\n127\t            shutil.rmtree(staged, ignore_errors=True)\n128\t\n129\t    caido_endpoint = await session.resolve_exposed_port(_CONTAINER_CAIDO_PORT)\n130\t    scheme = \"https\" if caido_endpoint.tls else \"http\"\n131\t    host_caido_url = f\"{scheme}://{caido_endpoint.host}:{caido_endpoint.port}\"\n132\t    logger.debug(\"Caido host endpoint resolved: %s\", host_caido_url)\n133\t\n134\t    caido_client = await bootstrap_caido(\n135\t        session,\n136\t        host_url=host_caido_url,\n137\t        container_url=container_caido_url,\n138\t    )\n139\t\n140\t    bundle = {\n141\t        \"client\": client,\n142\t        \"session\": session,\n143\t        \"caido_client\": caido_client,\n144\t    }\n145\t    _SESSION_CACHE[scan_id] = bundle\n146\t    logger.info(\"Sandbox session for scan %s ready and cached\", scan_id)\n147\t    return bundle\n148\t\n149\t\n150\tasync def cleanup(scan_id: str) -> None:\n151\t    \"\"\"Tear down ``scan_id``'s container and drop its cache entry.\n152\t\n153\t    Best-effort: any error during ``client.delete`` is logged and\n154\t    swallowed. We never want a cleanup failure to prevent the next\n155\t    scan from starting; the worst case is a stranded container that\n156\t    Docker's normal reaping will catch on next ``docker prune``.\n157\t    \"\"\"\n158\t    bundle = _SESSION_CACHE.pop(scan_id, None)\n159\t    if bundle is None:\n160\t        logger.debug(\"cleanup(%s): no cached session\", scan_id)\n161\t        return\n162\t\n163\t    caido_client = bundle.get(\"caido_client\")\n164\t    if caido_client is not None:\n165\t        try:\n166\t            await caido_client.aclose()\n167\t        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n168\t            logger.debug(\"cleanup(%s): caido_client.aclose() raised\", scan_id, exc_info=True)\n169\t\n170\t    client = bundle[\"client\"]\n171\t    try:\n172\t        await client.delete(bundle[\"session\"])\n173\t        logger.info(\"Cleaned up sandbox session for scan %s\", scan_id)\n174\t    except Exception:\n175\t        logger.exception(\n176\t            \"cleanup(%s): client.delete raised; container may need manual reaping\",\n177\t            scan_id,\n178\t        )\n179\t\n180\t    docker_client = getattr(client, \"docker_client\", None)\n181\t    if docker_client is not None:\n182\t        try:\n183\t            docker_client.close()\n184\t        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n185\t            logger.debug(\"cleanup(%s): docker_client.close() raised\", scan_id, exc_info=True)\n186\t"}]},"uuid":"505cfb9c-1cfe-44fe-9a3b-9a9de76a1d89","timestamp":"2026-07-20T01:21:19.875Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"1b2ecda2-2402-4f7a-ac87-c44e2a2378fa","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"6045c1a8-7658-4674-addd-5bb7a43030a5","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"3bfd3d93-3d8c-4d5a-8244-149007afff7e","agentId":"ae42573080b47b5cd","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01UupfrYv69NP8XMXo19TpC8","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Sandbox backend registry — selected via STRIX_RUNTIME_BACKEND (default: docker).\"\"\"\n2\t\n3\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n4\t\n5\timport logging\n6\tfrom collections.abc import Awaitable, Callable\n7\tfrom typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any\n8\t\n9\t\n10\tif TYPE_CHECKING:\n11\t    from agents.sandbox.manifest import Manifest\n12\t\n13\t\n14\tlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n15\t\n16\t\n17\tSandboxBackend = Callable[..., Awaitable[tuple[Any, Any]]]\n18\t\n19\t\n20\tasync def _docker_backend(\n21\t    *,\n22\t    image: str,\n23\t    manifest: Manifest,\n24\t    exposed_ports: tuple[int, ...],\n25\t    bind_mounts: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,\n26\t) -> tuple[Any, Any]:\n27\t    \"\"\"Bring up a session backed by the local Docker daemon.\n28\t\n29\t    Uses :class:`StrixDockerSandboxClient` to inject NET_ADMIN /\n30\t    NET_RAW caps + ``host.docker.internal`` host-gateway. Imports\n31\t    ``docker`` lazily so deployments that target a non-Docker\n32\t    backend don't need the docker-py library installed.\n33\t\n34\t    ``session.start()`` is what materializes the manifest entries\n35\t    (LocalDir copies and manifest-declared volume/FUSE mounts) into the\n36\t    running container — the SDK's ``client.create()`` only builds the inner\n37\t    session object without applying the manifest. ``async with session:``\n38\t    would call it too, but Strix manages session lifetime explicitly via\n39\t    ``client.delete()`` so we trigger ``start()`` ourselves.\n40\t\n41\t    ``bind_mounts`` are host directories (e.g. large repos passed via\n42\t    ``--mount``) bind-mounted read-only; unlike manifest entries they are\n43\t    applied by Docker at container-create time, not by ``start()``.\n44\t    \"\"\"\n45\t    import docker\n46\t    from agents.sandbox.sandboxes.docker import DockerSandboxClientOptions\n47\t\n48\t    from strix.runtime.docker_client import StrixDockerSandboxClient\n49\t\n50\t    client = StrixDockerSandboxClient(docker.from_env())\n51\t    client.strix_bind_mounts = bind_mounts or []\n52\t    options = DockerSandboxClientOptions(image=image, exposed_ports=exposed_ports)\n53\t    session = await client.create(options=options, manifest=manifest)\n54\t    await session.start()\n55\t    return client, session\n56\t\n57\t\n58\t_BACKENDS: dict[str, SandboxBackend] = {\n59\t    \"docker\": _docker_backend,\n60\t}\n61\t\n62\t\n63\tdef get_backend(name: str) -> SandboxBackend:\n64\t    \"\"\"Return the backend factory for ``name`` or raise.\n65\t\n66\t    Args:\n67\t        name: Backend identifier (e.g. ``\"docker\"``). Match is exact;\n68\t            no fallback. Unknown values raise so config typos surface\n69\t            immediately instead of silently picking a default.\n70\t    \"\"\"\n71\t    backend = _BACKENDS.get(name)\n72\t    if backend is None:\n73\t        supported = \", \".join(sorted(_BACKENDS))\n74\t        raise ValueError(\n75\t            f\"Unknown STRIX_RUNTIME_BACKEND: {name!r} (supported: {supported})\",\n76\t        )\n77\t    logger.debug(\"Selected sandbox backend: %s\", name)\n78\t    return backend\n79\t\n80\t\n81\tdef register_backend(name: str, backend: SandboxBackend) -> None:\n82\t    \"\"\"Register a custom backend under ``name``.\n83\t\n84\t    Intended for downstream users who ship their own runtime — register\n85\t    before any ``session_manager.create_or_reuse`` call. Re-registering\n86\t    an existing name overwrites the prior entry.\n87\t    \"\"\"\n88\t    _BACKENDS[name] = backend\n89\t    logger.info(\"Registered sandbox backend: %s\", name)\n90\t\n91\t\n92\tdef supported_backends() -> list[str]:\n93\t    return sorted(_BACKENDS)\n94\t"}]},"uuid":"aa79991c-17f6-4352-b320-05ed9fac39a1","timestamp":"2026-07-20T01:21:20.331Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"6045c1a8-7658-4674-addd-5bb7a43030a5","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"00af21d9-8784-48ad-bad8-4962bedc0f1a","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"3bfd3d93-3d8c-4d5a-8244-149007afff7e","agentId":"ae42573080b47b5cd","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01FfPPECL7qUP3zX3tWDhEsM","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Caido client bootstrap.\n2\t\n3\tThe Caido CLI runs as an in-container sidecar listening on\n4\t``127.0.0.1:48080`` *inside* the sandbox. We grab a guest token by\n5\t``session.exec()``-ing curl from inside the container, then construct\n6\ta host-side :class:`caido_sdk_client.Client` against the runtime's\n7\texposed-port URL for all subsequent SDK calls.\n8\t\"\"\"\n9\t\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\timport asyncio\n13\timport contextlib\n14\timport json\n15\timport logging\n16\tfrom typing import TYPE_CHECKING\n17\t\n18\tfrom caido_sdk_client import Client, TokenAuthOptions\n19\tfrom caido_sdk_client.types import CreateProjectOptions\n20\t\n21\t\n22\tif TYPE_CHECKING:\n23\t    from agents.sandbox.session import BaseSandboxSession\n24\t\n25\t\n26\tlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n27\t\n28\t\n29\t_LOGIN_AS_GUEST_BODY = (\n30\t    '{\"query\":\"mutation LoginAsGuest { loginAsGuest { token { accessToken } } }\"}'\n31\t)\n32\t\n33\t\n34\tasync def _login_as_guest(\n35\t    session: BaseSandboxSession,\n36\t    *,\n37\t    container_url: str,\n38\t    attempts: int = 10,\n39\t) -> str:\n40\t    \"\"\"``session.exec`` curl to fetch a guest token; retry until ready.\n41\t\n42\t    Caido's GraphQL listener may not be up the instant the container\n43\t    starts. The retry loop also doubles as the Caido readiness probe —\n44\t    no separate TCP healthcheck needed.\n45\t    \"\"\"\n46\t    last_err: str | None = None\n47\t    for i in range(1, attempts + 1):\n48\t        result = await session.exec(\n49\t            \"curl\",\n50\t            \"-fsS\",\n51\t            \"-X\",\n52\t            \"POST\",\n53\t            \"-H\",\n54\t            \"Content-Type: application/json\",\n55\t            \"-d\",\n56\t            _LOGIN_AS_GUEST_BODY,\n57\t            f\"{container_url}/graphql\",\n58\t            timeout=15,\n59\t        )\n60\t        if result.ok():\n61\t            try:\n62\t                payload = json.loads(result.stdout)\n63\t                token = (\n64\t                    payload.get(\"data\", {})\n65\t                    .get(\"loginAsGuest\", {})\n66\t                    .get(\"token\", {})\n67\t                    .get(\"accessToken\")\n68\t                )\n69\t                if token:\n70\t                    return str(token)\n71\t                last_err = f\"loginAsGuest returned no token: {payload}\"\n72\t            except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:\n73\t                last_err = f\"unparseable response: {exc}: {result.stdout!r}\"\n74\t        else:\n75\t            stderr = result.stderr.decode(\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\")[:200]\n76\t            last_err = f\"curl exit {result.exit_code}: {stderr}\"\n77\t        logger.debug(\"loginAsGuest attempt %d/%d failed: %s\", i, attempts, last_err)\n78\t        await asyncio.sleep(min(2.0 * i, 8.0))\n79\t\n80\t    raise RuntimeError(f\"loginAsGuest failed after {attempts} attempts: {last_err}\")\n81\t\n82\t\n83\tasync def bootstrap_caido(\n84\t    session: BaseSandboxSession,\n85\t    *,\n86\t    host_url: str,\n87\t    container_url: str,\n88\t) -> Client:\n89\t    \"\"\"Connect to the in-container Caido sidecar and select a fresh project.\"\"\"\n90\t    logger.info(\"Bootstrapping Caido client (host=%s, container=%s)\", host_url, container_url)\n91\t\n92\t    access_token = await _login_as_guest(session, container_url=container_url)\n93\t\n94\t    client = Client(host_url, auth=TokenAuthOptions(token=access_token))\n95\t    await client.connect()\n96\t\n97\t    try:\n98\t        project = await client.project.create(\n99\t            CreateProjectOptions(name=\"sandbox\", temporary=True),\n100\t        )\n101\t        await client.project.select(project.id)\n102\t    except BaseException:\n103\t        # The connected client never reaches the session bundle if project\n104\t        # setup fails, so close it here to avoid leaking the transport.\n105\t        with contextlib.suppress(Exception):\n106\t            await client.aclose()\n107\t        raise\n108\t    logger.info(\"Caido project selected: %s\", project.id)\n109\t    return client\n110\t"}]},"uuid":"c098d177-24cd-4e3b-b8e7-33388107be3e","timestamp":"2026-07-20T01:21:26.481Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"00af21d9-8784-48ad-bad8-4962bedc0f1a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
